- **source**: Use when a key must be born from randomness you can prove — seed quality is established at the source and across the fleet, never by inspecting the seed. A CSPRNG on a weak seed is indistinguishable from one on a strong seed, so no per-seed statistical test appears here by design. attest is an HMAC under a key only genuine hardware holds; admit refuses a forged tag, a short seed, and the same seed arriving from a second device (the deterministic-RNG fingerprint no single device can see). No key derives from an un-admitted seed. ([erpax/erpax](https://github.com/erpax/erpax/tree/HEAD/src/entropy/source) · ★2)
